158 円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 
～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の"三つの分岐点"が方向性を決める～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ ドル円相場は、7 月末の日米協調介入、9 月上旬の急激な円高、9 月の日米同時利上げを経て、
10 月に入ってからは1 ドル＝158 円前後の狭いレンジでの推移が続いている。円先物市場に
おける非商業部門の建玉は、9 月前半に過去に例のない規模で円の売り越しから買い越しへ
と転じた。しかし、その後は買い越し幅が縮小し、売建玉が再び増え始めている。投機筋の
円買い意欲は弱まりつつあると言えよう。 
○ 期待インフレ率 （BEI） で実質化した日米の実質金利差は、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債のいず
れでも 2％ポイントを超える米国優位が続いている。名目金利差は縮小したものの、米国の
金利上昇は実質金利の上昇、日本のは期待インフレ率の上昇によるところが大きい。このた
め、実質ベースのドル優位は崩れていない。 
○ 当面の焦点は、①11 月 3 日の米中間選挙、②選挙後のイラン情勢、③日銀の 12 月利上げと
その後のペース、の三つである。民主党が上下両院を制し、イラン情勢が沈静化に向かい、
日銀が利上げ路線を明確にすれば、円高方向への転換が視野に入る。逆の組み合わせとなれ
ば、再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が強まる展開も否定できない。

1. 投機筋の円ポジションは"円売り一色"から変化し、158 円を挟んで膠着が続くドル円相場 
ドル円相場は、方向感を欠いた推移が続いている。10 月入り後の値動きは、高値が158.94 円
（10 月14 日） 、安値が156.99 円（10 月2 日）と、月間の値幅は2 円弱にとどまる。 
もっとも、ここに至るまでの道のりは平坦ではなかった。4 月末から5 月の大型連休中には、
160 円台後半まで進んだ円安に対して、政府・日銀が総額11.7 兆円の円買い介入を実施した。そ
れでも円安基調は止まらず、7 月 28 日には163.91 円（ECB参照値）と、約40 年ぶりの円安水準を
つけた。これに対し、7 月 31 日には日米当局が28 年ぶりとなる円買い協調介入に踏み切った。介
入額は 15 兆3993 億円に達し、年初からの累計は27 兆 1342 億円と暦年で過去最大を更新した。 
その後は円高へと自律的な反発も見られた。9 月2～3 日には、日銀の追加利上げ観測の強まり
や米雇用関連指標の下振れを受けてドル円は1 日で3 円以上下落、9 日には153 円台まで円高が進
んだ。この局面では介入実績は無く、ジャクソンホール会議でのウォーシュFRB議長の発言をきっ

### Page 2

2/6 
かけに積み上がったドル買い・円売りの持ち高が、損切りを迫られた結果と考えられる。 
ところが、その後は再び円安に押し戻された。FRBは 9 月 16 日に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切
り、政策金利を3.75～4.00％とした。日銀も18 日に政策金利を1.25％へ引き上げたものの、日
米がそろって利上げしたことで金利差は大きく変わらず、ドル円は158 円台へと戻っている。 
為替市場における"投機的な動き"の指標とされるのが、円先物市場における非商業部門（主に
ヘッジファンドなどとされる）の建玉である。その推移を見ると、ここ数ヶ月で大きな変化が生
じていることがわかる（図表1）。 
6 月 2 日時点の円売建玉は24 万 4416 枚と、円キャリートレードがブームだった2007 年以来の
高水準に達していた。差引（ネット）でも12 万9567 枚の売り越しだった。その後も、9 月1 日時
点では 9 万2227 枚の売り越しと、円売り超の状態が続いていた。 
しかし、9 月前半の急激な円高を境に状況は一変し、9 月 15 日時点では差引が12 万 0359 枚の
買い越しへと転じ、わずか2 週間で円買い方向に21 万枚強も振れた。この振れ幅は、統計をさか
のぼれる1986 年以降で最大である。同時点の円買建玉（23 万 7951 枚）も過去最大を記録した。 
もっとも、その後は買い越し幅の縮小が続いている。10 月13 日時点では、買建玉19 万 1256 枚
に対し売建玉14 万 1049 枚で、差引の買い越しは5 万0207 枚とピークの半分以下に縮んだ。売建
玉は 9 月末の12 万枚台から14 万枚台へと再び増えており、円売りの持ち高が積み上がり始めて
いる兆しもうかがえる。投資主体別に見ると、レバレッジド・ファンドは10 月 13 日時点で3 万
3673 枚の円売り越しとなっており、ヘッジファンドの円買い意欲がすでに後退していることを示
している。 
足元の売建玉は、過去の介入局面のように当局が介入を急がなければならない水準にはない。
一方で、9 月に積み上がった円の買い持ちの解消そのものが、円安圧力として作用している面もあ
ると考えられる。

3/6
2.  実質金利差から見たドル円相場
為替相場の方向性を最終的に決めるのは、日米の金融政策と、それを反映した金利差である。
そこで、日米の実質金利差を、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債の3 つの年限で見てみる（図表2） 。こ
こでは、実質化に用いる物価として、市場が織り込む期待インフレ率であるBEI（ブレーク・イー
ブン・インフレ率、10 年債）を用いた。足元の消費者物価は、米国では原油高で押し上げられ、
日本では電気・ガス料金支援で押し下げられており、実勢を見誤りやすいためである。
名目で見ると、政策金利は米FF金利の誘導目標が3.75～4.00％、日本の無担保コール翌日物が
1.25％である。10 月 21 日時点の国債利回りは、米国が2 年債4.79％・10 年債 5.13％、日本が2
年債 1.886％・10 年債2.882％となっている。一方、10 年BEIは米国が2.35％（10 月20 日） 、日
本が 2.1～2.2％程度と、ほぼ同水準である。これを差し引いて実質化すると、筆者の試算ではお

おむね次のようになる。
✓ 政策金利：米国＋1.5～1.6％程度、日本▲0.9～1.0％程度（米国優位・差は約2.5％ポイント）
✓ 2 年債：米国＋2.4％程度、日本▲0.3％程度（米国優位・差は約2.7％ポイント）
✓ 10 年債：米国＋2.8％程度、日本＋0.7％程度（米国優位・差は約2.1％ポイント）
名目で見れば、日本の10 年債利回りが9 月下旬に3.115％と約 30 年ぶりの水準に達するなど、
日米金利差は縮小してきた。しかし、BEIで実質化すると景色は異なる。すべての年限で米国の実
質金利が2％ポイント以上も上回っており、ドル優位の構図はほとんど崩れていないのである。
その背景には、日米で金利上昇の"中身"が異なることがある。米国では、昨年末から長期金利
が約 1％ポイント上昇したが、BEIは 2.25％から2.35％へとほぼ横ばいにとどまった。名目金利の
注：BEIで実質化

4/6 
上昇は、ほぼそのまま実質金利の上昇となり、10 年物の実質利回りは9 月末に 2.93％と2008 年
以来の高水準に達した。これに対し日本では、BEIが過去最高圏の2％台まで上昇しており、名目
金利の上昇の相応の部分を期待インフレ率の上昇が占めている。実際、9 月の日銀の利上げ後も
BEIはむしろ上昇した。日本の金利上昇は"インフレ期待による金利上昇"の色彩が濃く、実質金利
の上昇は限られていると言えよう。 
日本の政策金利は、実質では依然として▲1％近いマイナス圏にあり、金融環境はなお緩和的
だ。日銀の利上げや介入にもかかわらず円安の流れが変わらなかったのは、こうした実質金利差
の構図で説明できる面が大きい。逆に言えば、日本の期待インフレ率が2％近辺で落ち着いたまま
利上げが進むか、米国の実質金利が低下に転じない限り、本格的な円高への転換は難しいと考え
られる。 
なお、日本の物価連動国債は発行残高が約10 兆円と小さく、BEIには流動性の影響も含まれ
る。政策金利や2 年債を10 年BEIで実質化するのも簡便的な手法である点には留意が必要だ。

3.  中間選挙の結果次第で変わるドルの行方 
11 月3 日には米国の中間選挙が行われる。1934 年以降の23 回の中間選挙のうち、現職大統領
の政党が上下両院とも議席を増やしたのはわずか2 回しかない。今回も、ロイター通信の世論調
査ではトランプ大統領の支持率が過去最低の31％まで落ち込み、投票先では民主党47％、共和党
35％と 12 ポイントの差がついている。選挙予測サイトのディシジョン・デスクHQは、民主党が下
院を制する確率を78％、上院を制する確率を56％と見ている。 
結果ごとに、ドル円相場への影響を整理すると次のようになる。 
① 民主党が上下両院を制する場合：予算や減税、関税政策に対する議会のチェックが強まり、
財政拡張への期待が後退することで、米長期金利の低下とドル安につながりやすい。FRBの人事に
対する議会の影響力が強まり、金融政策の独立性への懸念が和らぐことも、長期金利の押し下げ
要因となろう。一方で、予算案を巡る対立が先鋭化すれば、政府機関の閉鎖などを通じたリスク
回避の円買いが生じる可能性もある。いずれにしても、ドル円には下押し圧力がかかる公算が大
きい。 
② 下院は民主党、上院は共和党の"ねじれ"となる場合：現在の政策の大枠が維持される一方
で、新たな大型財政出動は難しくなる。相場の反応は限定的で、関心は再び日米の金融政策へと
移ることになろう。 
③ 共和党が両院の過半数を維持する場合：減税や規制緩和など拡張的な政策への期待から、米
長期金利の上昇とドル高が進みやすい。ドル円が再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が一気に高
まる展開も想定される。 
世論調査どおりであれば①ないし②の公算が大きく、ドル円にはどちらかと言えば円高寄りの
材料となろう。ただし、重要なのは選挙後の財政・通商政策や金利見通しの変化であり、選挙直
後の値動きだけで方向性を判断するのは早計だろう。

### Page 5

5/6 
4.  イラン情勢：選挙後の"重し"が外れる 
もう一つの大きな不透明要因がイラン情勢である。2 月 28 日に始まった米国・イスラエルとイ
ランの戦闘は、ホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖と、米国によるイラン港湾の海上封鎖を伴ったまま
長期化している。北海ブレント原油先物は20 日に一時1 バレル＝111.99 ドルと約5 ヶ月ぶりの高
値をつけた。 
原油高は、いくつかの経路でドル高円安に作用している。米国では、ガソリン価格の前年比
34％の上昇などを背景に、9 月の消費者物価が同＋3.7％へと加速し、FRBの追加利上げ観測を支え
ている。日本にとっては、エネルギー輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支が悪化し、実需の円売りが
増える。加えて、地政学リスクの高まりは"有事のドル買い"も誘いやすい。 
注目されるのは、トランプ大統領が「中間選挙前にはイランを攻撃しない」と明言している点
だ。言い換えれば、選挙後はこの制約が外れることになる。選挙後に軍事行動が再開されれば、
原油価格の一段高と米利上げ観測の強まりを通じて、ドル高円安が進むリスクがある。逆に、仲
介国を通じた交渉がまとまり、海峡の再開に向かえば、原油価格の下落とともに米長期金利が低
下し、ドル安円高へと振れやすい。実際、20～21 日には原油安を受けて米長期金利が低下し、ド
ルが売られる場面が見られた。

5.  日米の金融政策：12 月の"同時利上げ"の先が焦点 
FRBは9 月に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切り、年内にもう1 回の利上げを示唆した。10 月14 日
に9 月の消費者物価が前年比3.7％と予想を上回ると、27～28 日のFOMCでの利上げ確率は一時5
割前後まで上昇した。しかし、17 日にウォーシュ議長が10 月会合の判断を予断しない姿勢を示し
たことで、利上げ確率は1 割台半ばまで低下している。市場では「10 月は見送り、12 月に利上
げ」との見方が大勢となった。一部には、12 月が今回の利上げ局面で最後になるとの予想も出て
いる。 
一方、日銀の植田総裁は、9 月の利上げ後に「政策の局面は変化した」と述べ、その後も基調的
な物価上昇率が2％に「かなり近づいている」との認識を示した。そのうえで、 「これ以上上がる
ことは容認できない」とも踏み込んでいる。29～30 日の決定会合では政策金利が据え置かれる公
算が大きいが、基調物価が2％に到達したと判断されると報じられている。 
日米がともに12 月に利上げすれば、名目の政策金利差は変わらない。為替にとって重要なの
は、その先の方向性の違いである。FF金利が明確に中立水準を超えるFRBの利上げが最終局面に近
づく一方、日銀は中立金利を目指して利上げを続ける。この構図が鮮明になれば、日米の実質金
利差は徐々に縮小に向かおう。逆に、原油高を受けて米国の物価上昇が加速し、FRBが一段の利上
げを迫られる展開となれば、実質金利差の縮小は見込めず、ドル円相場の膠着状態を演出しよ
う。

6.  今後の見通し：年末に向けて緩やかな円高を想定、ただし分岐は大きい 
以上を踏まえると、当面のドル円相場は、日米の金融政策会合と中間選挙という大きなイベン

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
トを控え、当面は現状の狭いレンジでの推移が続くと考えられる。10 月27～28 日のFOMCは据え置
きが市場の大勢だ。FOMCで利上げが見送られ、その後の日銀会合で基調物価2％到達が確認されて
12 月利上げへの地ならしが進めば、円高方向に振れやすい組み合わせとなる。しかし、市場では
FRBも年内利上げを実施するとの見方が強く、ドル円相場を動かす材料とはなりにくい。逆に、
FRBが10 月のFOMCで利上げを決定した場合、ドル円相場は159 円を超える円安進展の可能性が高
まる。 
年末にかけては、①中間選挙で民主党が議会の主導権を握り、②イラン情勢が交渉による解決
に向かい原油価格が落ち着き、③日銀が12 月に利上げを実施して来年以降も利上げ継続の姿勢を
示す、という三つの条件がそろえば、日米の実質金利差は縮小に向かう。その場合は、150 円台半
ばから前半への円高が進む可能性がある。投機筋の売建玉が再び積み上がっている局面であれ
ば、その巻き戻しが円高の勢いを増幅することも考えられる。 
一方で、①共和党の善戦、②選挙後のイランへの軍事行動の再開、③FRBの利上げ継続＋日銀の
利上げ慎重姿勢打ち出し、といった組み合わせとなれば、ドル円は再び160 円台を試すことにな
ろう。その場合、政府・日銀は過去の介入水準である160 円台前半から164 円近辺を意識しつ
つ、円安のスピードを抑えるための介入に踏み切る公算が大きい。ただし、これまでの経験が示
すように、ファンダメンタルズが変わらない中での介入は"時間を買う"政策にすぎず、効果が一
時的にとどまる可能性には注意が必要だ。 
筆者は、①米中間選挙は最低でも上下院のねじれ、場合によっては上院においても民主党の過
半数獲得、②選挙後に米国がイランへの軍事行動を再開、同時に仲介国を介した交渉を再開する
ものの、膠着状態が続く、③日米とも利上げ継続、をメインシナリオとしている。 
さらにリスク要因として、①米中間選挙の結果がなかなか確定せず、議会勢力の確定に時間が
かかる、②イラン情勢はフーシ派から攻撃を受けているサウジアラビアや、総選挙を終えたイス
ラエルによる本格的な攻撃参戦、の可能性を挙げる。 
このように不透明要因が多いことも足元のドル円相場の膠着の要因になっていると考えられる
が、短期的な値動きに一喜一憂するよりも、実質金利差という基本的な決定要因がどちらに動い
ているかを冷静に見極めることが重要であり、日米中央銀行の金融政策姿勢が最も注目される。

以 上

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI 從 8 月的 55.4 降至 54.9，顯示服務業活動增速放
緩，然而國內需求強勁使供應鏈承壓，並推動投入價格指數由 72.6 升至 74.0 為
四年多來新高；另一方面，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心指數由 9 月的 48.1
降至 46.3，為連續第三個月下降且創 5 月以來最低 ， 惟未來一年通膨預期由 4.6%
升至 4.7%，顯示通膨可能持續維持高位至 2027 年。
⚫ 聯準會 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示與會官員均支持升息，並認為年底前尚需升息
一次 ，但對於後續升息時點及升息理由上存在分歧； 其中部分官員認為升息是防
止能源及其他價格衝擊造成更廣泛影響 的預防性措施，但更為鷹派官員則認為
升息在於抑制投資和支出，以防範正在顯現的需求驅動型通膨。
⚫ 歐洲央行 9 月貨幣政策會議紀要顯示全體決策委員均同意升息，並認為當前經
濟成長風險偏向下行 ， 通膨風險則偏向上行 ，因此將保留政策彈性， 根據經濟數

據做為決策，不提供未來利率路徑指引。
⚫ 台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨警
戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ，除了豪雨導致蔬果價格上漲及中秋假期的短期因
素外，油價居高不下亦使通膨壓力增加。
⚫ 中國央行發布匯率政策立場聲明， 強調中國貿易發展根源於產業國際競爭力的
提升，無必要通過匯率貶值獲取貿易競爭優勢 ，並指出中國長期實施以市場供需
為基礎、參考一籃子貨幣進行調節管理浮動匯率制度。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.699，低點在 31.940，以 31.875 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 4.5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，受到前週公布的美國非農就業數據低於預期，降低
市場對聯準會 10 月升息的預期， 台股創高吸引外資匯入 ， 推升台幣最高來到31.699；
不過隨著美伊緊張情勢升溫且台灣連假將至 ， 台股收低使外資轉為大幅賣超並匯出 ，
推低台幣回到 9 月中以來的震盪區間下緣，最低觸及 31.940，最終在央行調節之下
收在 31.875。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心
指數持續下滑，由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創 5 月以來最低，惟未來一年通膨預期
升至 4.7%，加上中東局勢反覆，油價高位震盪，仍支撐市場對於聯準會年內升息
的預期，預計美元指數維持在 102 附近震盪，對台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台灣 9 月出口創歷史新高，年增 60.9%，反映 AI 及科技需求持續強勁，出口商後
續拋匯需求可望提供台幣支撐；然而，台股逼近五萬點關卡之際，外資未能延續買
超走勢，且連假前已大幅賣超台股，若後續外資持續匯出，將限制台幣升值空間。
綜合以上，預估本週台幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.700-32.000

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣區間震盪 。 中國十一長假期間 (10/1~10/7) 境內人民幣市場休市 ， 而
境外人民幣維持偏強格局 ， 主要在6.7000 上方震盪 ， 臨近周末 ， 人行中間價為6.7330，
創 3 年多來新高，加上美元指數漲勢放緩，推升境外人民幣突破 6.7000 整數關卡，
終場境外人民幣收在 6.6927，週線升值 134 點，境內人民幣收在 6.6929，較十一長
假前夕升值 121 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間偏升。就國際市場來看，美國密西根大學消費者信心指數
由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創5 月以來新低 ， 不過對於未來一年的通膨預期升至4.7%，
加上油價維持高檔 ， 市場對於聯準會年內升息的預期仍存 ， 美元指數維持高檔震盪 ，
將限制人民幣升值速度；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價維持偏強設定，不過人
行表示將防範短期大幅波動與急貶風險 ， 並無意透過人民幣貶值取得貿易競爭優勢 ，
有助於穩定市場對人民幣的預期 ；最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 各天期
均線仍向下發散，空頭格局不變，下方支撐先看向 9 月底低點 6.6908。綜合以上，
預估本週人民幣區間偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6800-6.7100

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪走升。週初，因美元指數在102 附近盤整，帶領USD/JPY 延
續前一週走勢震盪走升到 158 上方；週間，因缺乏重要經濟數據，市場偏向觀望，
USD/JPY 維持在 158 附近震盪；臨近週末，USD/JPY 曾一度回測 157.50 附近，不
過在美日利差的主導下，USD/JPY 依舊保持偏多的走勢，終場收在 158.31，週線上
漲 84 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、路透短觀調查顯示，受半導
體強勁訂單推動，日本 10 月大型製造業信心升至正 22，創近五年新高；但原料成
本高漲重擊非製造業，信心指數降至正 23。與此同時，此前投下升息反對票的日銀
鴿派委員佐藤綾野立場軟化，表態支持分階段升息，市場對後續正常化預期升溫，
預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間 。二 、 日本首相高市早苗表示密切關注匯率與物價
走勢 ， 強調日本已脫離通縮 ， 將完全尊重央行政策獨立性 ， 暗示不反對進一步升息 。
面對通膨加劇與日圓走貶壓力，其過往堅持的貨幣寬鬆立場已出現轉向。然而目前
美日實質利差仍大，持續為 USD/JPY 提供支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，
USD/JPY 持續沿著上彎的月線上攻，然匯價持續承壓於季線下方，在有效站上季線
及突破前高壓力前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震
盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.10-159.50。

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 震盪 走跌 。週初， 因法國債券風險與區域政治不確定性 ，
EUR/USD 下行最低來到 1.1160； 週間 ，受中東地緣風險升溫與歐洲財政疑慮帶動避
險需求 ， 加上美債殖利率維持高檔 ， 美元利差優勢延續，歐元維持低檔； 臨近週末 ，
逢低歐元有買盤支撐，帶動歐元收復部分跌幅，終場收在 1.1201，週線下跌 46 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學
家連恩表示，夏末能源成本飆升為經濟增長帶來下行風險，並可能限制央行收緊貨
幣政策的力道，與此同時，歐元區 10 月 Sentix 投資者信心指數由 5.1 降至 2.7，德
國預期指數同步滑落，反映市場對經濟復甦的信心明顯降溫，預計將令歐元持續承
壓。二、法國社會動盪與支出訴求阻礙財政減赤進程，加劇債務可持續性疑慮，並
推升法德公債利差與信用風險，令歐元承壓走弱。相較美國經濟展現韌性且聯準會
維持偏鷹立場，歐洲深陷景氣放緩與財政掣肘，歐洲央行緊縮空間受限。在歐美基
本面與政策前景持續分歧下 ，EUR/USD 缺乏實質支撐 ， 後市將維持偏弱整理並下探
關鍵支撐。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖多次向下測試 1.1165 關
鍵價位均獲得支撐，惟每次反彈均無法站上五日線，且各天期均線下彎呈現空頭排
列。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1100-1.1260

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週邁入新的提存期 ， 央行連日減發存單 ， 令市場資金供
給充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週一央行標售 364 天期定存單 1100 億元 ， 加權平均得標利率為1.602%， 低於
市場預期區間 1.61%-1.635%，較上期標售結果下跌 4.1 個基本點。 
週 四 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 受美債利率續創新高影響，週四 10 年期指標券 A15109 成交在 2.05%，創同
年期券自 2008 年以來高點，5 年期指標券 A15108 則未成交，最近成交利率
為 1.82%。 
台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨
警戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ， 目前油價仍居高難下 ， 通膨壓力將令央行維
持偏緊的貨幣政策，加上國際債市利率持續走升，台債殖利率恐面臨上行壓
力，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.95%，10 年券區間為 2.0%~2.10%。

Gigabyte (2376.TW): AI servers to drive 2H26 growth; Capex 
commitments remain strong; Neutral
20 October 2026 | 10:16PM CST
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Oct-Dec on a 
YoY basis, driven by rising AI server racks, leading our 4Q26 revenues YoY to 
accelerate to 79% (vs. 60% / 41% YoY in 1Q26 / 2Q26). However, we expect revenue 
decline in Oct-Dec on a MoM basis which we attribute to AI servers model transition 
and the high base in Sep; nevertheless, we continue to expect revenues sequentially 
increase qoq in 4Q26E. We expect the stronger growth in 2H26 to be driven by 
ongoing AI spending, customers’ faster AI deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding 
customer base and enhancing backlog visibility. Customers’ infrastructure readiness 
and components procurement should continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the 
procurement on AI servers. The company maintains strong capex commitment, and 
we expect 2H26 capex to stay at a similar high level vs. 1H26, leading 2026 capex to 
grow at 148% YoY to NT$2.9bn to capture the strong AI servers demand, and 
customers’ requirements on diversifying production sites (e.g., Gigabyte is across 
Taiwan, US, Europe, and Southeast Asia).

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$492. Our TP is 
based on a 14.2x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 20 Oct 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 3

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Long-term strategies: Resources concentrated on ABF/ AI PCB — As we
highlighted, Unimicron has been converting BT capacity into ABF, which currently
causes some BT capacity to be idle during the transition period (negative impact will
likely last into 1H27, in our view). Thus, currently, we are less bullish on its BT GM
recovery in the near term vs other BT peers. However, we expect this conversion will

help the company to secure more ABF demand earlier considering customers
currently are still waiting for those ABF greenfield capacity expansion projects to
come in the next few years. For HDI/mSAP , Unimicron would only focus on AI
server/optical module products in the long term. To note that, the company is still
less aggressive in HDI capacity expansion given higher competition from Chinese
peers. Meanwhile, the company is also downsizing its traditional PCB capacity (i.e.,

for non-AI business) and just announced its plan to sell its PCB plant in Japan to
other players. We expect there will be more downsizing plans to come for traditional
PCB capacity.
GM expansion on track; reiterate Buy — We raise our 2026/27 /28E by 10%/5%/5%
to factor in robust GM expansion by ABF. We remain positive on its GM expansion by
ABF and also see potential upside coming from HDI and BT segments. We raise our
DCF-based TP to NT$1,550 (30x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 and reiterate our Buy

rating.

### Page 6

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Unimicron
Company description
Established in 1990, Unimicron is the world's No.1 PCB maker and HDI board
maker. After the acquisition of PPT in late 2009, its revenue exposure to
substrate has surpassed 40%, with the rest almost equally shared by
conventional PCB and HDI. Unimicron has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
China (Suzhou, Kunshan, Shenzhen). UMC is the largest shareholder, with a
~13% stake.

Investment strategy
We rate Unimicron shares a Buy. Our positive view is premised on: more
upside potential in the ABF segment thanks to product-mix improvements,
better pricing on more complex product upgrade, and capacity expansion.
Despite macro uncertainty, from a long-term perspective, we believe the
tightness in ABF is unlikely to ease without much new capacity ramped
aggressively in the upcoming years, and Unimicron should be the largest
beneficiary with its scale in ABF. Thanks to AI GPU/ASIC demand, we now see

strong growth in 2026/27E ABF consumption. We believe Unimicron could
see strong revenue growth again as it did in 2019–21 from both ABF new
capacity and a BT rebound.
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,550. We use a 1-year forward-looking
DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect Unimicron's ability to generate
stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 5.0% (10-year government bond rate), a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share

price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 14.6%. Our target price is equivalent
to 62x/30x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus
PE multiple of the past five years. However, as the market focuses on 2027
upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI business; 2) significant increase

in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure
from depreciation due to higher capex investments.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26E

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +39% QoQ, driven by: (1) AI data center: The 
next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp-up, with higher ASP and early 
delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first to deliver the latest model, around 2-3 
months ahead of peers, driving its Oct and onward revenue growth. Followed by 
GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks should fuel the growth 
in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27. As our Global Server TAM 
update shows, we expect the AI server rack global value TAM to grow at 163% CAGR in 
2025-28E to US$1tn, or 63% of the global server value TAM by 2028E. And (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlighted in September (report link), 
we reduced our global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m units to 1.1bn / 
1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we 
remain positive on the potential for global leading brands to outperform the market 
given their strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger 
consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium 
models to continue to gain market share, which should be positive to Hon Hai. Maintain 
Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect the company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +61% 
YoY/ +39% QoQ to NT$4.2 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 47%, 
or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both YoY and 
MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new smartphone 
models, and CPO switches. Management noted that AI-related products remain on track 
to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products continue to 
benefit from typical 2H seasonal tailwinds, which should support overall operational 
performance in line with current consensus expectations.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI servers ramp-up to drive growth ahead; model transition and PC 
shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26

Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entered quarter-end at a strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, riding 
on a reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramp up, along with 
growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better specification and high 
raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 4% QoQ, considering the 
high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp-up of new AI server racks. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA 
and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 2026E-28E along with the specification 
upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading US CSPs aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion

Wiwynn delivered strong Sep revenues, which were 19% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general server demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI server ramping up. We expect revenue YoY growth to remain strong at mid 
double-digit through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +60 YoY / +8% QoQ, 
driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM update), we raised AI servers implied AI chips shipment by 9% / 
19% in 2027 / 28E with ASIC penetration rate increasing to 58% by 2028E, and our US 
internet team forecasts that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will 
grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our US CSP capex 
estimates raised by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, supporting 
our positive view on Wiwynn. Maintain Buy with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$4,000

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 20.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.  
Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): AI server racks to see strong ramp-up with customer expansion and 
specification upgrades

Wistron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY to NT$381bn, or 18% lower than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the impact of shipment timing volatility in higher-ASP AI products. 
We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI 
server racks ramp-up in 4Q26, which should drive up the ASP , along with strong AI 
spending and expansion of the company’s customer base. The new GPU AI server racks 
have various racks across GPU, CPU, and LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming 
quarters. Sep PC shipment was up 7% MoM but down 32% YoY to 1.5m units, reflecting 
the slow PC demand amid the consumption pull-in under the high memory cost. Despite 
PC shipments growth likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in since late 
2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, driving 2H26 
revenue up +41% HoH. Maintain Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$291 (12.6x 2027E P/E 
vs. 30.4% NI YoY in 2027-28E).

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$61.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance.

2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

(2) broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on 
Pegatron’s expansion from consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive

Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY / 143% MoM to NT$215bn, or 64% higher than 
our estimate. This reflects a strong ramp-up in AI servers in Sep. Sep PC shipments were 
up c.8% MoM but down 44% YoY to 0.46m, reflecting the weak PC demand under the 
high memory costs. Prior to September, the company suffered from model transition (i.e. 
declining demand for older models while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up 
phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 4% / 6% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratio. We raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 16% / 28% / 27%, considering the AI server shipment ramp-up 
and higher ASP on better specification. Given the much lower GM of AI server racks 
compared to Pegatron’s other products, our changes in gross profits narrow to 9% / 12% 
/ 15% in 2026E-28E. The opex ratio for 2026-28E is cut by 0.1 ppt/ 0.2 ppt/ 0.1 ppt to 
reflect the improved operational efficiency.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 GM Dragged by Inventory
Write-off
Despite the secular tailwinds of increasing power consumption
in AI clusters, we think near-term sentiment toward Delta could
remain lackluster post weaker 3Q26 results.
3Q26 earnings fell short on lower GM:
• Off revenue of NT$197,059mn (+8% QoQ/+31% YoY), GM contracted  by
1.6ppt QoQ to 34.1% (vs. MSe/consensus at 35.7%/36.2%), which was
dragged 2ppt lower by the inventory write-off

• Opex was well controlled at NT$34,535mn (opex ratio 17.5%), lower than our
estimate of NT$36,983mn (opex ratio 18.4%) and consensus of NT$37,129mn
(opex ratio 18.0%)
• Operating profit came in at NT$32,620mn (+7% QoQ/+31% YoY; OPM
16.6%), 7% lower  than MSe of NT$34,992mn (OPM 17.4%) and 13% lower
than consensus of NT$37,677mn (OPM 18.2%).
• With non-op gains of NT$1,046mn (including FX gain of NT$415mn), net
profit was NT$23,495mn (-7% QoQ/+26% YoY; EPS NT$9.04), 8%/15% short

of MSe/consensus at NT$25,554mn (EPS NT$9.84)/NT$27,611mn (EPS NT
$10.40).
Our view:
• We believe near-term sentiment toward the stock will stay soft, though the
weak financial performance in 3Q26 was mainly impacted by component
supply tightness
• We believe Delta should grow its revenue by 10% QoQ with margin
expansion in 4Q26, and will continue to benefit from the increasing power
consumption of AI clusters in 2027
• Earnings call at 3pm HKT on Oct 29th  (details here)

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28 EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 63% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 170: 35 岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了

Source PDF: all report pdf/35 岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做成上线系统的⼈。
摘要：
数据显⽰，全球科技公司年内已宣布裁员近  20 万⼈。与此同时，有⼀个  AI 岗位的招聘量却在
⼀年⾥暴涨了  7 倍多，薪资中位数折合⼈⺠币约  130 万元。

OpenAI 、 Anthropic 、微软、亚⻢逊、⾕歌，⼏家巨头前后砸下近百亿美元，有的开新公司，有
的建新部⻔，抢的都是这类⼈才。最反常识的是：这个岗位最稀缺的本事，恰恰不是写代码。

Anthropic ：培养  1 万个 “ 把  AI 送进企业的⼈ ”

10 ⽉  2 ⽇， Claude 的开发商  Anthropic 宣布投⼊  1 亿美元成⽴ “Claude 前沿学院 ” （ Claude
Frontier Academy ），⽬标是在  2027 年底前培养  1 万名 “ 前沿部署⼯程师 ” 。

⾸批学员是从合作伙伴中选出的在职⼯程师，来⾃埃森哲、摩根⼠丹利、诺和诺德和⼏家顶级
咨询公司。培训分两段：先是  4 天线下集训，从⼀次 “ 模拟企业部署 ” 开始；考核通过后，学员回
到⾃⼰公司，带⼀个真实项⽬做  12 周实战。⾸批认证预计在  2027 年初完成。

在  Anthropic 看来，企业落地  AI 的瓶颈不在模型，⽽在于缺少能推动变⾰的⼈。负责这个项⽬
的⾼管  Steve Corfield 说，客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做
成上线系统的⼈。

这只是今年  AI 巨头抢⼈⼤战中的最新⼀例。

砸下百亿美元，巨头们都在抢同⼀种⼈才

今年  5 ⽉， OpenAI 成⽴了专⻔帮企业落地  AI 的 “OpenAI 部署公司 ” ，由  TPG 领投，⾸轮融资
超过  40 亿美元。同⽉， Anthropic 与⿊⽯、 Hellman & Friedman 、⾼盛合资成⽴了⼀家  AI 服
务公司，各⽅初始投资约  15 亿美元。

6 ⽉底到  7 ⽉初，亚⻢逊  AWS 和微软先后出⼿： AWS 投⼊  10 亿美元，组建⾃⼰的部署⼯程
团队；微软投⼊  25 亿美元成⽴新部⻔，把约  6000 名⼯程专家派驻到客⼾企业。 9 ⽉，⾕歌云
和埃森哲成⽴联合业务部⻔，计划培训最多  1000 名埃森哲⼯程师，专⻔派驻客⼾现场。

过去这些公司⽐的是谁的模型更强、参数更⼤、跑分更⾼，如今却不约⽽同地把钱砸向同⼀个
⽅向：派⼈进客⼾公司，把  AI 真正⽤起来。

这类⼈才在业内有个统⼀的叫法：FDE （ Forward Deployed Engineer ），中⽂常译作“ 前线部
署⼯程师 ”。

招聘量三年猛涨 30 倍，年薪中位数超百万元

熟悉美股的读者，对这个词的源头不会陌⽣。 FDE 最早是  Palantir （帕兰泰尔）在⼗多年前系
统化推⾏的⼀种做法：⼯程师不坐在总部写通⽤产品，⽽是常驻客⼾现场，和客⼾⽤同⼀套数
据、扛同⼀个业务指标，把模糊的需求、分散的数据和落不了地的⽬标，⼀点点变成真正能⽤
的系统。

当年很多⼈觉得这种模式太重、太慢，不像⼀家软件公司该⼲的事。今天回头看，这恰恰是
Palantir 最难被复制的护城河： 2026 年第⼆季度， Palantir 营收  19.35 亿美元，同⽐增⻓
93% ，其中美国商业收⼊同⽐增⻓  149% 。公司⾸席执⾏官  Alex Karp 有⼀个在业内被反复引⽤
的判断：⼤模型当然重要，但⾄少在未来⼏年⾥，价值在于落地实施。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

据劳动⼒市场分析机构  Lightcast 统计， 2023 年全美  FDE 招聘⼴告不到  200 条， 2025 年约
1200 条， 2026 年仅前九个⽉就已超过  7300 条。这些⼴告标出的薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元，
⽐  2023 年⾼出  40% ，是全美所有招聘⼴告薪资中位数的三倍多。 Indeed 的数据也指向同⼀个
⽅向：美国  FDE 职位从  2025 年  4 ⽉的  643 个增⾄  2026 年  4 ⽉的  5330 个，⼀年涨了  7 倍
多。

值得注意的是，今年前九个⽉发布  FDE 招聘⼴告最多的雇主中，德勤、埃森哲等咨询公司排在
了⾕歌等科技巨头之前。可⻅  FDE 已不只是  AI 公司的内部技术岗位，任何想把  AI ⽤进业务的
企业都需要这样的⼈。

需要说明的是， FDE 不等于 “ 驻场外包 ” 。外包是甲⽅说什么就做什么；FDE 则要先判断⼀件事
该不该⽤  AI 、⽤哪个模型、花多少钱、怎么证明有效，再把它真正落地上线。它要解决的是企
业⾥的⼀个⽼难题：懂技术的不懂业务，懂业务的不懂技术。

AI 落地难在业务，稀缺的恰恰是懂业务的⼈

很多⼈看到这⾥会想：这是年轻程序员的机会，我不写代码，也过了转⾏的年纪。

但不妨看看  Anthropic 怎么描述⼀个好的  FDE ：熟悉  Agent 系统、擅⻓解决问题、善于沟通、
有⼯程经验。这四条⾥，有⼀半在技术之外。

过去两年，⼏乎每家企业都买过  AI 产品、做过  AI 试点，但真正跑进⽇常业务的远⽐宣传的
少。⼏组被业内⼴泛引⽤的数据或许能说明⼀些问题：标普全球的调查显⽰，企业  AI 项⽬的弃
⽤率⼀年内从  17% 升到了  42%； Gartner 预计，到  2027 年超过四成的  Agent 项⽬会被取消；
IDC 与联想  2025 年的⼀份报告则显⽰，88% 的  AI 概念验证项⽬最终没有投⼊⽣产。

问题往往不出在模型上 —— 演⽰时⼀切顺利，⼀进⼊真实业务，就会碰上脏数据、⽼系统、权
限审批、员⼯习惯，以及 “ 出了错谁负责 ” 。毕⻢威的⼀位⾸席技术架构师举过⼀个例⼦：⼀个应
⽤先花了⼏个⽉梳理需求和业务背景，真正⽤  AI 开发只⽤了⼤约⼀个⽉。

FDE 真正值钱的那⼀半能⼒不是编程，⽽是懂业务。⼀份合同哪⼏条要紧，⼀张报表哪个数字
会出事，⼀个审批流程卡在哪⼀步，哪件事交给机器做会让⽼板睡不着觉，这些  AI 替代不了，
反⽽是在⾏业⾥⼲了⼗⼏、⼆⼗年的⼈最清楚。

你缺少的不是经验，⽽是把经验交给  AI 去执⾏的那⼀步：知道今天的  AI 能做什么、不能做什
么、该怎么搭、要花多少钱、出了错怎么兜底。

我们这次的训练营，就是为这⼀步准备的。

两天⾛完  AI 落地最后⼀公⾥

华尔街⻅闻⾸期「 FDE 实战训练营」将于  11 ⽉  14 ⽇⾄  15 ⽇在上海开班。这是⼀个线下实战
营，限量  50 席，在两天时间⾥，带你从零做出⼀个真正能⽤、能上线的  AI Agent 。

先说说  Agent （智能体）是什么。很多⼈对  AI 的印象还停留在 “ 问⼀句答⼀句 ” 的聊天上，但
Agent 不⼀样：你交给它⼀件事，它会⾃⼰查资料、打开软件、读写⽂件、交出结果，做错了还
能⾃⼰返⼯。巨头们派  FDE 进企业，部署的正是这种技术。

两天训练营共  12 个模块，从模型怎么选、价格怎么算，⼀直讲到评测、成本优化和安全上线，
都是⼤⼚把  AI 做上线时真正在⽤的⽅法。主讲讲师  Right 曾任腾讯⾼级算法⼯程师，主导过微
信核⼼  AI Agent 项⽬的算法设计和规模化落地，在  Agent 系统设计、模型训练、 Harness 架构
⽅向有深厚积累。他还在  NeurIPS 、 ACL 等顶级会议发表过多篇  Agent ⽅向论⽂，相关⼯作被
Google DeepMind 引⽤。

课程适合谁
企业主和管理者：想弄清  AI 到底能替公司做哪些事、每件事花多少钱、⻛险在哪⾥

### Page 3

⾦融、法务、财务、运营、咨询等专业⼈⼠：⼿上有⼤量重复⼯作，想亲⼿做⼀个⼯具交给
AI
想在  AI 时代给⾃⼰多留⼀项硬技能的⼈

三⼗年前，先学会⽤  Excel 的那批⼈，在后来的⼆⼗年⾥少熬了⽆数个夜。
今天巨头们花百亿美元在抢懂业务、⼜会⽤  AI 的⼈，⽽你已经有了⼀半。剩下那⼀半，就在这
两天⾥补⻬。

時碩 (4566  TT)  Trading Buy

2H26 營運明顯增長 ，投資建議 Trading Buy 。  
投 資 建 議 Trading Buy ： 雖 目 前 時碩 (4566 TT) 處於 CB 緘 默 期 ，
未 提 供 明 確 展 望 ， 但 受 惠 於 航 太 、 散 熱 產 品 出 貨 增 長 ，3Q26 營
運 已 明 顯 攀 升 。 考 量 2026 、 2027 年 營 運 動 能 向 上 ， 投 資 建 議
Trading Buy ， 目 標 價 92 元 (以 202 7 年 EPS 及 PE  17 倍 評 價 )。  
時 碩 積 極 布 局 高 成 長 潛 力 的 利 基 型 市 場 ， 包 含 液 冷 散 熱 系 統 、
人 形 機 器 人 零 組 件 ： 液冷散熱 方面， 時 碩 致 力 於 提 供 一 站 式 的
全 方 位 熱 管 理 解 決 方 案 ， 其 產 品 線 涵 蓋 快 接 頭 、 分 歧 管 、 冷 卻
液 分 配 單 元 、 微 通 道 板 及 技 術 門 檻 較 高 的 水 冷 板 ， 並 能 提 供 不
鏽 鋼 與 銅 等 多 樣 材 質 。公司 已 成 功 獲 得 美 、 日 系 液 冷 系 統 大 廠
訂單，且於 2H26 放量 出貨 ， 預估 散 熱 產 品 佔 202 6 年 營 收 比 重
有望 接近 10% 。 人 形 機 器 人方 面 ， 時 碩 積 極 拓 展 此 領 域 的 關 鍵
零 組 件 業 務 ， 產 品 涵 蓋 諧 波 減 速 機 關 鍵 零 組 件 、 機 器 手 臂 關 節
精 密 機 械 加 工 零 件 。 時 碩 目 前 已 打 入 美 國 與 中 國 廠 商 的 供 應 鏈
體 系 ； 中 國 無 錫 廠 藉 由 地 緣 優 勢 積 極 切 入 關 節 零 件 製 造 。 泰 國
春 武 里 新 廠也 規 劃 將 產 線 逐 步 延 伸 至 機 器 人 相 關 應 用 領 域 。 
3Q26 營 收 明 顯 躍 升，預估 2026 、2027 年 營 運 動 能 向 上：雖 1H26
獲 利 表 現 平 平 ， 但 受 惠 於 航 太 、 散 熱 應 用 出 貨 明 顯 增 長 帶 動 ，
3Q26 營收 18.18 億元， QoQ+23.8 6%、 YoY+43.23% ； 且 出 貨 動
能 有 望 延 伸 至 4Q26 。2026 年 方 面 ，汽 車 、 航 太 、 工 業 三 大 領 域
皆有望較 2025 年 增 長。汽 車 應 用 方 面， 雖 中 國 市 場 競 爭 仍 然 激
烈，但 因 切 入 新 能 源 車 領 域 且 客 戶 需 求 帶 動 下，營 收穩 定 增 長。
航太領域 方面， 天 陽 航 太 已 通 過 多 項 航 太 認 證 ， 成 功 打 入 全 球
前 兩 大 飛 機 製 造 商 及 Tier 1 供 應 商 ， 預 估 營 收 將維 持 高 速 成 長
態勢 。 工 業 應 用 方 面 ， 既 有 領 域 有 望 較2025 年 改 善 ， 且 新 成 長
動 能 將 來 自 於 液 冷 散 熱 相 關 產 品 及 人 形 機 器 人 零 組 件 。 預 估
2026 年 營 收 66.00 億 元 ，YoY+ 32.62 %， 毛 利 率 維 持 高 檔 ， 營 業
利益 YoY+ 97.15 %。 預 估 稅 後 純 益 3.71 億 元 ， YoY+ 148.83 %，
EPS 3.36 元。 隨 著 航 太 、 散 熱 應 用 持 續 發 酵 ， 且 泰 國 新 廠 建 置
完 成 ， 預 估 2027 年 營 收 、 獲 利 動 能 向 上 。

  時碩 (4566  TT) E SG ：  
時碩的 ESG 政 策 由 「 永 續 發 展 暨 風 險 管 理 委 員 會 」 統 籌 ， 並 由 「 風 險 管 理
推 動 委 員 會 」 負 責 執 行 與 追 蹤，將 氣 候 變 遷 及 各 項 永 續 議 題 納 入 公 司 整 體 策
略 與 風 險 管 理 流 程 中。在 環 境 面，公 司 依 循 國 際 標 準 進 行 溫 室 氣 體 盤 查 與 第
三 方 確 信 ， 並 透 過 節 能 設 備 導 入 、 能 源 管 理 與 監 測 系 統 、 製 程 優 化 、 太 陽 能
及綠電等多元措施推動減碳與能源效率提升，同時強化水資源與廢棄物管
理 ， 藉 由 回 收 再 利 用 、 廢 水 循 環 及 資 源 化 設 備 ， 落 實 減 量 及 循 環 經 濟 ； 原 物
料 與 包 材 則 遵 循 RoHS 、REACH 等 規 範，降 低 製 程 對 環 境 的 衝 擊。社 會 面 方
面，時 碩 重 視 員 工 薪 資 福 利、培 訓 與 職 涯 發 展，推 動 性 別 平 等 與 管 理 階 層 多
元 化，並 建 立 職 業 安 全 衛 生 制 度 及 教 育 訓 練，以 降 低 職 災 風 險；在 供 應 鏈 則
透 過 定 期 稽 核 與 評 分 機 制，提 升 供 應 商 在 品 質、交 期 與 服 務 上 的 表 現，並 以
產 品 品 質 與 安 全 為 核 心 ， 確 保 生 產 與 產 品 符 合 相 關 要 求 。 同 時 ， 公 司 以「 企
業 公 民 」 自 我 定 位，投 入 社 會 公 益 與 在 地 關 懷，減 少 營 運 對 社 區 的 負 面 影 響，
並 維 持 完 善 的 資 訊 安 全 管 理 以 避 免 資 訊 外 洩。在 治 理 面，時 碩 建 構 包 含 獨 立
董 事 與 女 性 董 事 的 董 事 會 結 構，設 置 薪 酬 與 審 計 等 功 能 性 委 員 會，訂 定 涵 蓋
營 運 、 財 務、 法 遵 、 環 境 與 氣 候 變 遷 、 人 資 與 人 權 等 面 向 的 風 險 管 理 政 策 ，
並 透 過 法 說 會、網 站 與 永 續 報 告 與 利 害 關 係 人溝 通，同 時 積 極 參 與 各 項 ESG
獎 項 評 選 ， 以 強 化 公 司 治 理 與 永 續 形 象 。

6 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다.

표 2. 삼성바이오로직스 중장기 투자 계획 (단위: 조 원) 
항목 예상투자비 예상 투자기간 비고 
PolyPeptide 인수 2.71 2026년 CHF 1,462,957,171.41 기준 
6공장 증설 1.9 2027~2029년 180kL Capa 확장 투자 (바이오리액터, 정제설비 등) 
제3바이오캠퍼스 증설 7.0 2027~2034년 주력 항체 이외 차세대 Modality 확장 관련 생산 설비 도입

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

Document 268: A Fed credibility-induced bounce

Source PDF: all report pdf/A Fed credibility-induced bounce.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Weekly Market Views)
Date: 2026-09-18
Pages: 15
Status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office 
18 September 2026

A Fed credibility- 
induced bounce

 The Fed’s unanimous decision to 
hike rates this week for the first time 
since 2023 and pencil in another hike 
by December confirms our 
expectations of a central bank willing to 
establish its independence against 
political pressure. 
 The BoJ also hiked rates this week. 
It is the first time on record all three 
major central banks – Fed, ECB and 
BoJ – have hiked in the same month, 
as they respond to a resurgence in 
energy-driven inflation amid tight job 
markets. 
 We expect the Fed to hike once 
more by year-end to 4.25% and follow 
up with another hike in H1 2027 to 
4.5%. With the revised estimates, we 
revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts.  
 Nevertheless, with Fed policy 
uncertainty easing, we see an 
opportunity to use the equity market dip 
to add exposure amid solid corporate 
earnings.

What is the outlook for US 
government bond yields 
following the Fed meeting? 
Will the step-up in the Fed’s 
risk-free rate trigger a repricing 
of equities? 
What is the impact of BoJ and 
BoE policy meetings on their 
respective currencies?

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

Standard Chartered Bank

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is it still appropriate to have a positive stance on US 
high-yield (HY) bonds? 
Our view: We remain opportunistically bullish on US HY bonds. 
Lower sensitivity to interest rate volatility, a below-trend default 
rate and a maturity wall pushed out to 2028-29 drive this view. 
Rationale: Recent interest rate volatility has highlighted a key 
structural advantage of US HY bonds: their higher coupon rates  
and shorter tenors relative to Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds and US investment-grade (IG) corporate bonds translate into 
a much lower sensitivity to interest rates, cushioning bond prices 
during yield backup episodes, where bond yields rise sharply. 
YTD, US HY has delivered 1.8% in total returns, outperforming DM 
government bonds and US IG corporate bonds by over 3% each on 
a relative basis. This outperformance was particularly evident during 
the recent back-up in bond yields, as inflation concerns re-emerged 
after renewed Middle East tensions – an environment where HY's 
lower rate sensitivity provided a relative cushion  even as broader 
fixed income markets came under pressure in absolute terms. On 
the fundamentals side, US HY corporate credit quality remains 
resilient. Default rates continue to track below long-run trend levels, 
and the maturity wall has been pushed out to 2028 -29, reducing 
near-term refinancing risk. Against a non -recessionary macro 
backdrop, we expect these credit fundamentals to hold, supporting 
the case for continued opportunistic exposure to the asset class.  
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Do the latest BoJ and BoE meetings alter your view on the 
central banks’ rate paths and their corresponding currencies? 
Our view: We remain constructive on the JPY and expect two 
BoJ rate hikes this year and potentially one hike per quarter in 
H1 2027 as policy normalisation continues. Meanwhile, the BoE 
held rates at 3.75%. We expect GBP/USD to stay rangebound 
within 1.32-1.36 over the next 1-3 months, with downside risk 
towards the lower end of our medium-term 1.30-1.36 range. 
Rationale: Persistent inflation, higher energy prices and earlier JPY 
weakness support further BoJ policy normalisation . Continued 
hiking should progressively narrow the US -Japan rate gap, reduce 
the attractiveness of JPY -funded carry trades and support  the JPY 
over the medium term towards the 153-155 range. However, with a 
September hike largely priced  in, future tightening ’s pace  will be 
more important for the JPY. Meanwhile, the BoE was split 6-3 in its 
decision to hold rates at 3.75% , with three members preferring a 
25bps hike, signalling elevated inflation risks. However, a weaker 
labour market is likely to limit the pace of BoE tightening. The US-
UK rate divergence is expected to favour the USD over the next 1-3 
months. Further BoE tightening (if inflation remains elevated ) could 
narrow the rate gap and provide support to the GBP later in the cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

We expect US HY bonds to continue delivering 
strong performance in the near term

Weekly Economics
U.S. Week Ahead
Existing Home Sales • Thursday
Housing market conditions remain highly unfavorable. Mortgage
rates are the primary headwind, and they have climbed even higher
alongside the rise in longer-term yields. At the same time, low
supply is preventing a broad correction in home prices, leaving
prospective buyers squeezed on two fronts.
We expect that existing home sales slid 3.4% in August to an
annualized rate of 3.92 million. Behind this forecast is ongoing

weakness in mortgage applications and a recent bounce in
mortgage rates, which rose from 6.43% at the beginning of July to
6.66% by month-end.
We do not expect affordability conditions to meaningfully improve
in the near term. Supply is likely to remain constrained while high
mortgage rates discourage mobility. And mortgage rates are apt
to remain elevated amid firm economic growth expectations and
heavy government and corporate debt issuance.

Economics Week Ahead Economics
CPI • Friday
Headline inflation looks to have picked up last month. We estimate
the CPI rose 0.40% in August, with gasoline prices increasing a little
over 4% as renewed tensions in the Middle East pushed oil higher.
Grocery prices also likely rebounded after modestly declining in July.
Outside of food and energy, price pressures appear little changed.
We expect the core CPI to rise 0.23%, essentially matching July's

pace. Core goods inflation should remain firm, led by another solid
increase in used vehicle prices as consumer prices continue to
catch up to the trend indicated at wholesale auctions. Meantime,
household furnishings and other tariff-sensitive categories appear
to be stabilizing after declining earlier this year.
Core services inflation should be little changed with another 0.2%
monthly increase. But underneath the surface, we expect a modest
pickup in travel (led by lodging away from home) to offset slightly

cooler medical care and housing cost growth.
All told, we expect the report to show that while headline inflation
remains influenced by the ebbs and flows of the conflict in the
Middle East, core inflation remains contained. Should our forecast
prove correct, core CPI will ease to 2.4% year-over-year, in line with
its 6-month annualized rate but above the more recent 3-month
annualized pace of 1.7%.

### Page 4

Weekly Economics
G10 Week Ahead
European Central Bank Monetary Policy Meeting •
Thursday
We expect the European Central Bank (ECB) to raise the Deposit
Rate by 25 bps to 2.50% next week. August headline inflation
remained elevated at 3.3% year-over-year, though softer core
and services inflation suggest underlying pressures continue to
moderate. Still, the more hawkish tone of the July minutes and
renewed rise in energy prices should keep policymakers cautious.

The recent escalation in the Middle East has pushed fuel prices
at the pump close to their 2022 peaks, while natural gas storage
remains below historical levels, leaving the Eurozone vulnerable
to further energy price increases. The ECB is unlikely to respond
aggressively if the shock remains confined to energy given the
downside risks to growth. However, a broader rise in food inflation
linked to El Niño would be more concerning, as highly visible prices

could lift inflation expectations and generate second-round effects.
For now, resilient Q3 activity, elevated headline inflation and
contained wage growth support one final hike. The ECB will likely
retain and emphasize its scenario-based outlook in the updated
staff projections given continued uncertainty around the conflict
and energy prices. The policy path beyond September will depend
on whether energy and food pressures persist and begin to spread,

which could leave room for further tightening.

U.K. Monthly GDP • Friday
We expect UK GDP to rise 0.2% month-over-month in July, with
growth of 0.3% on a three-month-over-three-month basis.

Activity likely moderated after June’s strong rebound, though
services remained supportive as the July services PMI rose to 52.1.
Retail sales volumes fell 0.5% month-over-month, while softer
manufacturing activity likely reflected some reversal of earlier
precautionary stockbuilding.
The moderation appears more consistent with payback from
June than a broader deterioration in momentum. August PMIs
remain consistent with Q3 GDP growth of around 0.3% quarter-

over-quarter. Still, the renewed escalation in the Middle East and
associated rise in gas and energy prices adds upside risk to inflation
and could weigh on household demand. Against this backdrop and
with Bank of England hawks continuing to emphasize the need for
tighter policy, we expect the BoE to remain cautious and continue
to look for a rate hike in Q4. -1.0%

Economics Week Ahead Economics
EM Week Ahead
Mexico CPI • Wednesday
We expect Mexico’s August inflation rate to edge higher, with
headline CPI rising to 3.34% year-over-year (0.27% month-over-
month) from 3.26% year-over-year in early August. Part of the
pickup likely reflects less favorable base effects and a smaller
disinflationary contribution from food prices. While agricultural
prices continued to help restrain inflation in mid-August, the drag

was notably less pronounced than in July. Core inflation should
remain elevated at 3.98% year-over-year (0.25% month-over-
month).
While inflation remains within Banxico’s midpoint 3% target,
persistent core and services inflation suggest convergence toward
the midpoint of the target could prove gradual. For now, we
continue to expect Banxico to keep rates on hold, with the policy
rate remaining at 6.50% through the rest of 2026 and into early
2027 as policymakers await clearer evidence that underlying

inflation pressures are easing sustainably. While rate cuts remain
a possibility in 2027, the current inflation backdrop argues for
patience in the near term.

Brazil CPI • Friday
We expect Brazil’s August inflation rate to slow to 4.20% year-over-
year, helped by lower electricity, food and fuel prices. The main
driver should be a temporary reduction in residential electricity bills
stemming from a one-off credit, while recent data already point to
a notable easing in price pressures. Mid-August CPI came in weaker
than expected, with inflation falling to 4.24% year-over-year amid
declines in electricity, food, fuel and airfare prices. Beyond these

temporary factors, restrictive monetary policy continues to weigh
on economic activity, as evidenced by the recent slowdown in Q2
GDP growth. However, underlying inflation risks remain. A still-
tight labor market and government measures aimed at supporting
consumption could keep services inflation sticky, making progress
toward the 3.0% inflation target more gradual.
Overall, the inflation outlook remains consistent with additional
policy easing. We expect COPOM to deliver a 25 bps rate cut in

September, bringing the Selic Rate to 13.75%. Beyond September,
policymakers are likely to proceed cautiously as they assess
incoming inflation data and the impact of the election cycle on
inflation expectations. Our base case remains for a pause after
September, with additional easing more likely in 2027.

Document 331: Energy shock_ Dashboard 2026 vs. 2022 - October 2026 (2026-10-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Energy shock_ Dashboard 2026 vs. 2022 - October 2026 (2026-10-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas

### Page 1

Energy shock: Dashboard 2026 vs. 2022
10/2/2026 2
Will the same causes produce the same effects? In other words, will the war in Iran and the resulting
surge in oil and gas prices lead to an inflationary shock comparable to that seen in 2022? Will their
negative effects on growth be the same as those of the war in Ukraine and the subsequent energy
shock?
We have selected a set of indicators to track the impact of this new energy shock — caused by the war

in the Middle East — on activity and prices in the Eurozone, the United States, oil and gas markets and
emerging countries, and to see how much the current situation resembles that of 2022 at the outbreak
of the conflict in Ukraine.
This dashboard features charts and comments that will be updated on a monthly basis for as long as
necessary.
Overall, based on data available through September 2026, economic activity continues to show
resilience. The high levels of business climate surveys illustrate this well. Household confidence,

however, is more vulnerable and sensitive to energy price pressures. Financing conditions in emerging
economies also continue to hold up well against the shock (much better than in 2022) as well as
against the tensions in DM bond markets, which intensified this summer, partly in the wake of the
reinforcing upward pressure on energy prices. Higher energy prices are also fueling inflationary
pressures and pushing inflation even higher. In particular, Eurozone inflation rose by 0.6 percentage

points to 3.8% year-on-year (September flash estimate), reaching its highest level in three years.
ECONOMIC RESEARCH
Eurozone: Marked increase in inflation in September 2026,
to a 3-year high
Source: S&P Global, Eurostat, European Commission, Macrobond, BNP Paribas
Change since m = 0 = February 2026 / February 2022. Unit: inflation change in percentage point, point for confidence surveys.

Document 335: Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-02

### Page 1

September 2026: Marked improvement in
services business confidence
September 2026: Business climate in the
manufacturing sector comfortably in the
expansion zone for the 8th month in a row
September 2026: Strengthening inflationary

September 2026: Marked increase in inflation,
to a 3-year high
Eurozone: Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer

confidence falls back
02/10/2026 3
We have selected two inflation measures (with and without energy) and six survey indicators: 1/ & 2/ business confidence, as
measured by the PMIs in the manufacturing and services sectors; 3/ & 4/ input and output prices components of the
composite PMI (in order to identify direct inflationary pressures); 5/ the suppliers’ delivery times component of the
manufacturing PMI (a direct indicator of possible supply difficulties and supply-demand imbalance, and therefore, indirectly,

of inflationary pressures in the making); and 6/ consumer confidence (measured by the “assessment of financial situation in
the next 12 months” in order to capture the impact of inflation on purchasing power). The trends in each of these indicators
are observed relative to month m=0, corresponding to the start of the conflict. Each line does not represent the level of the
indicator, but its cumulative variation compared to month m=0.
The overall picture from the confidence surveys available for September is globally positive. According to the PMIs, the

business climate in services has indeed seen a clear improvement, and that in the manufacturing sector remains comfortably
in expansion territory for the 8 th month in a row. However, consumer confidence has recorded its first decline in 5 months,
likely driven by rising inflation and concerns over purchasing power. News on the inflation front is negative. PMI surveys
report a strengthening of inflationary pressures: this trend is not a surprise given the tensions in energy prices, but it is

nonetheless significant. As for inflation itself, it continues to rise, more markedly in September: +0.6 percentage points, to
3.8% year-on-year (Eurostat flash estimate), of which 0.4 pp du to the higher energy contribution. Inflation excluding energy
increased as well, by 0.2 pp to 2.3% (of which 0.1 pp due to the rise in food inflation).

Document 341: First Impressions_ NZ business confidence, October 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ business confidence, October 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf

### Page 1

#businessconditions #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ business confidence, October 2026
By Michael Gordon
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
12:17 October 29 2026 Share
Business confidence was steady in October, suggesting that firms are learning to live with
higher fuel prices.
Key results, October 2026
Business confidence: 52.5 (Prev: 51.9)
Expectations for own activity: 46.2 (Prev: 47.9)

### Page 2

Activity vs same month one year ago: 15.0 (Prev: 10.8)
Inflation expectations: 3.31% (Prev: 3.25%)
Pricing intentions: 52.2 (Prev: 48.4)

Business confidence remained high in the October survey. General business sentiment was up slightly
to 52.5 compared to September, while own-activity expectations were slightly lower at 46.2.
The most notable development in the last month has been the resurgence in fuel prices, with petrol
prices at the pump breaking above the previous record highs seen in the initial wake of the Iran conflict.
That was understandably reflected in the pricing measures of the survey, with cost expectations and
pricing intentions ticking higher. But the general steadiness in the activity measures suggests that
businesses are simply learning to live with higher fuel costs.
The proportion of businesses expecting higher costs, and planning to increase their own prices,
remains well above pre-Covid levels. However, the expected size of these increases remains relatively
muted, and lower than what we saw in the initial stages of the Iran conflict. This perhaps speaks to the
view of some of the RBNZ Monetary Policy Committee members (including Governor Breman) that the
extent of second-round inflation pressures is less than they initially feared. Actual and expected wage
growth also remains muted, although that’s unsurprising as this is typically a lagging indicator.
The ANZ business survey is one of the relatively limited number of major data releases ahead of the
RBNZ’s December policy review (the next survey will be released on 30 November). Today’s results
are unlikely to change minds one way or another – the hikers at yesterday’s OCR decision will note the
resilience of activity, while the holders will note the muted degree of cost passthrough. Our view
remains that the RBNZ is likely to deliver a 25bp OCR hike in December, but this is far from a done
deal.
